        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "### Arithmetic and comparisons
                  Each operator compiles its two operands by [llvmOperand] in turn, and then the instruction it
                  is ([llvmInstr]), given for integers and for floats.
                  # TODO An arm written as the call of a helper compiling both operands
                  #   (@/ContextIn(value= llvmBinary(op, a(..ps)= a(..ps), b(..ps)= b(..ps)), ctx)@) makes inference
                  #   of the core component run past 15 minutes, where these binds take seconds; each arm repeats
                  #   them until that is found."
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "An [LlvmBinOp] is an instruction of two operands."
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "## Operands"
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "An [LlvmType] is the type of an [Operand]: a 64-bit integer ([I64]), a double ([F64]), a
                  boolean ([I1]), or a 32-bit integer ([I32])."
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "## Compiling a function"
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /p -> ∀/Data/String =
                    ( /Data/String/join
                        ( EAppArg
                            { eaaArg = Value /sep ->
                                ( /Data/String/Text
                                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                ) = ","
                            }
                        )
                        ( EAppArg
                            { eaaArg = Value /parts -> /Data/List/Cons[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                                ( /head=∀/Data/String
                                , /tail=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                                ) =
                                ( /LLVM/llvmReversed
                                    ( EAppArg
                                        { eaaArg = Value /lines -> /Data/List/Cons
                                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                            , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                                [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                                    [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                    ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                    , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                            ( EAppArg
                                                { eaaArg = Value /head ->
                                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                    ) = "c"
                                                }
                                            )
                                            ( EAppArg
                                                { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                                                    [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                    ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                    , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                                        [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                        ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                        , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                                        )
                                                    ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                                    ( EAppArg
                                                        { eaaArg = Value /head ->
                                                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                            ) = "b"
                                                        }
                                                    )
                                                    ( EAppArg
                                                        { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                                                            [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                            , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                                            ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                                            ( EAppArg
                                                                { eaaArg = Value /head ->
                                                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                                    ) = "a"
                                                                }
                                                            )
                                                            ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil } )
                                                        }
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            )
                                        }
                                    )
                                    ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /acc -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil } ) ↦ /Data/List/Cons[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                                    ( /head=∀/Data/String
                                    , /tail=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                    )
                }
            )
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /result ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "a,b,c"
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "## Writing a module
                  The parts of a module, each written as the text it is in a `.ll` file."
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /p -> ∀/Data/String =
                    ( /LLVM/llvmModule
                        ( EAppArg
                            { eaaArg = Value /functions -> /Data/List/Cons[ /T=∀/Data/String ]
                                ( /head=∀/Data/String
                                , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                ( EAppArg
                                    { eaaArg = Value /head -> ∀/Data/String =
                                        ( /LLVM/llvmDefine
                                            ( EAppArg
                                                { eaaArg = Value /ret ->
                                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                    ) = "i32"
                                                }
                                            )
                                            ( EAppArg
                                                { eaaArg = Value /name ->
                                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                    ) = "main"
                                                }
                                            )
                                            ( EAppArg
                                                { eaaArg = Value /body -> /Data/List/Cons
                                                    [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                    ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                    , /tail=/Data/List/Cons
                                                        [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                        ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                        , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                                        )
                                                    ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                                    ( EAppArg
                                                        { eaaArg = Value /head ->
                                                            ( /Data/String/Text
                                                                ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                            ) = "entry:"
                                                        }
                                                    )
                                                    ( EAppArg
                                                        { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Cons
                                                            [ /T=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr ) ]
                                                            ( /head=( /Data/String/Text.. + /Catln/CStr )
                                                            , /tail=/Data/List/Nil
                                                            ) = /Data/List/Cons
                                                            ( EAppArg
                                                                { eaaArg = Value /head ->
                                                                    ( /Data/String/Text
                                                                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                                                                    ) = "  ret i32 0"
                                                                }
                                                            )
                                                            ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil } )
                                                        }
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                                        )
                                    }
                                )
                                ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /tail -> /Data/List/Nil = Value /Data/List/Nil } )
                            }
                        ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                    )
                }
            )
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /result ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                  define i32 @main() {
                  entry:
                    ret i32 0
                  }
                  "
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "## Compiling functions
                  What [llvm] compiles a function to, read as the text of the module it writes ([llvmText])."
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /p -> ∀/Data/String =
                    ( /LLVM/Example/llvmText
                        ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/arith = Value /LLVM/Example/arith } ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                    )
                }
            )
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /result ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                  define i64 @main() {
                  entry:
                    %r0 = mul i64 2, 3
                    %r1 = add i64 %r0, %x
                    ret i64 %r1
                  }
                  "
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /p -> ∀/Data/String =
                    ( /LLVM/Example/llvmText
                        ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/floats = Value /LLVM/Example/floats } ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                    )
                }
            )
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /result ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                  define double @main() {
                  entry:
                    %r0 = fmul double 1.5, 2.0
                    %r1 = fsub double %r0, 0.5
                    ret double %r1
                  }
                  "
                }
            )
        , /Catln/#print
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /p -> ∀/Data/String =
                    ( /LLVM/Example/llvmText
                        ( EAppArg { eaaArg = Value /c -> /LLVM/Example/less = Value /LLVM/Example/less } ) ↦ ∀/Data/String
                    )
                }
            )
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /result ->
                    ( /Data/String/Text
                        ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ).. + /Catln/CStr
                    ) = "; ModuleID = 'catln'

                  define i1 @main() {
                  entry:
                    %r0 = icmp slt i64 %x, 3
                    ret i1 %r0
                  }
                  "
                }
            )
        ]
    }
